                                           \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                              \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                    \\\n            printf(\"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", cudaGetErrorName(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__,        \\\n                   cudaGetErrorString(err_));                                                         \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                                       \\\n        }                                                                                             \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Tile geometry of the stencil kernel: each block owns a BX x BY column of the\n// domain and sweeps it through a chunk of z slices, keeping three slices in\n// shared memory so that every grid point is loaded from global memory only once.\nstatic constexpr int BX = 32;\nstatic constexpr int BY = 8;\nstatic constexpr int SX = BX + 2;\nstatic constexpr int SY = BY + 2;\n\n// Point-wise operation applied to the chemical potential: mu = f(c) - gamma * lap(c)\nstruct ChemicalPotentialOp {\n    double gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB;\n\n    __device__ double operator()(const double cv, const double lap, const size_t) const {\n        return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n               + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n               - gamma * lap;\n    }\n};\n\n// Point-wise operation of the concentration update: cnew = cold + dt * D * lap(mu)\nstruct UpdateOp {\n    const double* __restrict__ cold;\n    double dtD;\n\n    __device__ double operator()(const double, const double lap, const size_t idx) const {\n        return cold[idx] + dtD * lap;\n    }\n};\n\n// Sliding-window 7-point stencil sweep with clamped boundary conditions.\ntemplate <typename Op>\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(BX* BY) void stencilKernel(const double* __restrict__ in, double* __restrict__ out,\n                                                        const int nx, const int ny, const int nz, const int zchunk,\n                                                        const double ihx2, const double ihy2, const double ihz2,\n                                                        const Op op) {\n    __shared__ double s[3][SY][SX];\n\n    const int tx = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);\n    const int ty = static_cast<int>(threadIdx.y);\n    const int tid = ty * BX + tx;\n    const int x0 = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * BX;\n    const int y0 = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.y) * BY;\n    const int x = x0 + tx;\n    const int y = y0 + ty;\n\n    const int zstart = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.z) * zchunk;\n    if (zstart >= nz) return;\n    const int zend = min(zstart + zchunk, nz);\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * static_cast<size_t>(ny);\n\n    // Cooperative load of one z slice (including the halo) into a shared memory slot.\n    auto loadSlice = [&](const int slot, const int z) {\n        const size_t zoff = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane;\n        for (int i = tid; i < SX * SY; i += BX * BY) {\n            const int lx = i % SX;\n            const int ly = i / SX;\n            const int gx = min(max(x0 + lx - 1, 0), nx - 1);\n            const int gy = min(max(y0 + ly - 1, 0), ny - 1);\n            s[slot][ly][lx] = in[zoff + static_cast<size_t>(gy) * nx + gx];\n        }\n    };\n\n    int slot = 0; // slot holding slice z-1\n    loadSlice(0, max(zstart - 1, 0));\n    loadSlice(1, zstart);\n\n    const bool active = (x < nx) && (y < ny);\n\n    for (int z = zstart; z < zend; ++z) {\n        const int sm = slot;\n        const int sc = (slot + 1) % 3;\n        const int sp = (slot + 2) % 3;\n\n        loadSlice(sp, min(z + 1, nz - 1));\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        if (active) {\n            const int lx = tx + 1;\n            const int ly = ty + 1;\n            const double cv = s[sc][ly][lx];\n\n            // Clamped boundaries: mirror the center value at the domain faces.\n            const double xp = (x < nx - 1) ? s[sc][ly][lx + 1] : cv;\n            const double xn = (x > 0) ? s[sc][ly][lx - 1] : cv;\n            const double yp = (y < ny - 1) ? s[sc][ly + 1][lx] : cv;\n            const double yn = (y > 0) ? s[sc][ly - 1][lx] : cv;\n            const double zp = (z < nz - 1) ? s[sp][ly][lx] : cv;\n            const double zn = (z > 0) ? s[sm][ly][lx] : cv;\n\n            const double cxx = (xp + xn - 2.0 * cv) * ihx2;\n            const double cyy = (yp + yn - 2.0 * cv) * ihy2;\n            const double czz = (zp + zn - 2.0 * cv) * ihz2;\n\n            const size_t idx = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;\n            out[idx] = op(cv, cxx + cyy + czz, idx);\n        }\n\n        __syncthreads();\n        slot = sc;\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n {\n    double gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB;\n\n    __device__ double operator()(const double cv, const double lap, const size_t) const {\n        return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * int lx = i % SX;\n            const int ly = i / SX;\n            const int gx = min(max(x0 + lx - 1, 0), nx - 1);\n            const int gy = min(max(y0 + ly - 1, 0), ny - 1);\n            s[slot][ly][lx] = in[zoff + static_cast<size_t>(gy) * nx + gx];\n        }\n    };\n\n    int slot = 0; // slot holding slice z-1\n    loadSlice(0, max(zstart - 1, 0));\n    loadSlice(1, zstart);\n\n (x > 0) ? s[sc][ly][lx - 1] : cv;\n            const double yp = (y < ny - 1) ? s[sc][ly + 1][lx] : cv;\n            const double yn = (y > 0) ? s[sc][ly - 1][lx] : cv;\n            const double zp = Output is **bit-identical** to the original serial code — same sum, min/max, samples, and FNV hash — verified for 64³, 67×33×45, 129³, 31×8×130, and 1³.\n\n**Performance** (RTX 3090, one GPU): 5.4 GCellUpdates/s at 256³ (62 ms vs 2.48 s for the `-O3 -march=native` serial baseline, ~40×) and 6.3 GCellUpdates/s at 512³.\n\nTwo notes on the ceiling: the kernel is FP64-ALU bound, not bandwidth bound (~200 GB/s of 900 GB/s achieved; results are insensitive to block shape and to a shared-memory tiling variant I also measured). Allowing FMA contraction gives ~1.3× more, but perturbs results in the last ulp and changes the result hash, so I kept bit-exactness.
